[ASSISTANT]
The baseline passes with ADP = 968,044 (delay 910 ps, area 1063). The critical path is stage 2→3: exponent-diff subtract → clamp → 14-bit barrel shift → **17× 20-bit two's-complement (+1) adders** (a long ripple chain). Key insight: the per-lane negation `~x+1` can be replaced by XOR-folding rows plus a single popcount correction row in the CSA tree, and the mantissa "booth multipliers" are just 4×4 unsigned products (8-bit). Let me write a restructured design.
  -> create_file(filename='design1.v', content="// pdpu_top_pipelined -- 5-stage pipelined 16-lane posit dot-product-accumulate.\n// Bit-exact to the combinational reference pdpu_dot16 (ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation,\n// NaR handling identical). Restructured vs the baseline:\n//   * mantissa products are plain 4x4 unsigned multiplies (booth+CSA+add was\n//     equivalent to an exact 8-bit product)\n//   * per-lane two's-complement (+1) adders removed: aligned rows are XOR-folded\n//     and a single popcount correction row is folded into the compressor tree\n//   * the compressor tree lives in the alignment stage; stage 4 is only the\n//     final carry-propagate adder\n//   * register count strongly reduced (no mul_sum/mul_carry/aligned-row regs)\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);\n    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;\n    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] sel_nodes;\n    logic [(2**NumLevels)*NumLevels-1:0] index_nodes;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n    always_comb begin : flip_vector\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n        in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n      end\n    end\n    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n                index_lut[(k * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 == WIDTH - 1) begin : g_base\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 > WIDTH - 1) begin : g_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =\n              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n  end : gen_lzc\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule barrel_shifter #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;\n    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        if(MODE==1'b0) begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]<<(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]<<1 : temp_results;\n        end\n        else begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]>>(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]>>1 : temp_results;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(operand_i),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n        end\n    end\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    logic [n-2:0] value_after_round;\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n    assign value_after_round = value_before_round + round_value;\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = sign_i ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\n// balanced signed maximum tree\nmodule maxtree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 7\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] items,\n    output logic signed [W-1:0] mx\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign mx = $signed(items[0 +: W]);\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            wire signed [W-1:0] a = $signed(items[0 +: W]);\n            wire signed [W-1:0] b = $signed(items[W +: W]);\n            assign mx = (a > b) ? a : b;\n        end else begin : split\n            wire signed [W-1:0] ma, mb;\n            maxtree #(.N(NA), .W(W)) ua (.items(items[0 +: NA*W]), .mx(ma));\n            maxtree #(.N(NB), .W(W)) ub (.items(items[NA*W +: NB*W]), .mx(mb));\n            assign mx = (ma > mb) ? ma : mb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// balanced popcount tree\nmodule popcount #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17\n)(\n    input  logic [N-1:0] v,\n    output logic [pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1)-1:0] cnt\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign cnt = v[0];\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            assign cnt = v[0] + v[1];\n        end else begin : split\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NA+1)-1:0] ca;\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NB+1)-1:0] cb;\n            popcount #(.N(NA)) ua (.v(v[0 +: NA]), .cnt(ca));\n            popcount #(.N(NB)) ub (.v(v[NA +: NB]), .cnt(cb));\n            assign cnt = ca + cb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// greedy Wallace reduction of N rows (width W) down to two rows using 3:2\n// compressors (full adders, no serial carry chains). Depth = 6 for N=18.\nmodule csa_reduce #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 18,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 20\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] rows,\n    output logic [W-1:0] s_o,\n    output logic [W-1:0] c_o\n);\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : b1\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = '0;\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : b2\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = rows[W +: W];\n        end else begin : rec\n            localparam int unsigned M = N/3;\n            localparam int unsigned REM = N - 3*M;\n            localparam int unsigned NN = 2*M + REM;\n            wire [M*W-1:0] ts, tc;\n            genvar j;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : trip\n                wire [W-1:0] ra = rows[(3*j+0)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rb = rows[(3*j+1)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rc = rows[(3*j+2)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] ab = ra & rb;\n                wire [W-1:0] aob = ra | rb;\n                assign ts[j*W +: W] = ra ^ rb ^ rc;\n                assign tc[j*W +: W] = {((ab[W-2:0]) | (rc[W-2:0] & aob[W-2:0])), 1'b0};\n            end\n            wire [NN*W-1:0] nxt;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : pack\n                assign nxt[(2*j)*W +: W] = ts[j*W +: W];\n                assign nxt[(2*j+1)*W +: W] = tc[j*W +: W];\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < REM; j++) begin : pass\n                assign nxt[(2*M+j)*W +: W] = rows[(3*M+j)*W +: W];\n            end\n            csa_reduce #(.N(NN), .W(W)) sub (.rows(nxt), .s_o(s_o), .c_o(c_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i - es_i - 3;                  // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o - es_o - 3;                  // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1, EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MUL_W = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned CARRY_W = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);                 // 5\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_W = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;                  // 19\n    localparam int unsigned ASH_W = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);         // 4\n    localparam int unsigned NL = N + 1;                                     // 17 items/rows\n    localparam int unsigned ROW_W = SUM_W + 1;                              // 20\n\n    // ********************\n    // Stage 1: decode\n    // ********************\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    wire sign_acc;\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_acc;\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o)) u_dec_acc (\n        .operand_i(acc), .sign_o(sign_acc), .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc), .mant_norm_o(mant_acc)\n    );\n\n    generate\n        genvar gi;\n        for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi++) begin : g_dec\n            wire sg_a, sg_b;\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] re_a, re_b;\n            wire [MANT_WIDTH_I:0] mn_a, mn_b;\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_a),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_a), .mant_norm_o(mn_a)\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_b),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_b), .mant_norm_o(mn_b)\n            );\n            assign signs_a[gi] = sg_a;\n            assign signs_b[gi] = sg_b;\n            assign rg_exp_a[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_a;\n            assign rg_exp_b[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_b;\n            assign mants_a[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_a;\n            assign mants_b[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_b;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    wire [N-1:0] signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    wire [N:0] signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar gj;\n        for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj++) begin : g_eadd\n            assign rg_exp_c[gj*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $signed(rg_exp_a[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) +\n                $signed(rg_exp_b[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_exp;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_ma, pipe1_mb;\n    logic pipe1_sacc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_eacc;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_macc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs <= signs;\n        pipe1_exp   <= rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_ma    <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mb    <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_sacc  <= sign_acc;\n        pipe1_eacc  <= rg_exp_acc;\n        pipe1_macc  <= mant_acc;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Stage 2: multiply, exponent max, popcount\n    // ********************\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] prod;\n    generate\n        genvar gk;\n        for (gk = 0; gk < N; gk++) begin : g_mul\n            assign prod[gk*MUL_W +: MUL_W] =\n                pipe1_ma[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe1_mb[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [NL*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] items;\n    generate\n        genvar gl;\n        for (gl = 0; gl < N; gl++) begin : g_it\n            assign items[gl*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_exp[gl*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign items[N*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_eacc;\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] emax;\n    maxtree #(.N(NL), .W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_maxtree (.items(items), .mx(emax));\n\n    wire [CARRY_W-1:0] kpop;\n    popcount #(.N(NL)) u_pop (.v(pipe1_signs), .cnt(kpop));\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] pipe2_prod;\n    logic [NL*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe2_items;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_max;\n    logic [CARRY_W-1:0] pipe2_k;\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_macc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_prod  <= prod;\n        pipe2_items <= items;\n        pipe2_max   <= emax;\n        pipe2_k     <= kpop;\n        pipe2_signs <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_macc  <= pipe1_macc;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Stage 3: align, fold, compress\n    // ********************\n    // pre-alignment products, left justified in ALIGN_WIDTH bits\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] prod14;\n    generate\n        genvar gm;\n        for (gm = 0; gm < N; gm++) begin : g_p14\n            assign prod14[gm*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n                {pipe2_prod[gm*MUL_W +: MUL_W], {(ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_W){1'b0}}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign prod14[N*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = {1'b0, pipe2_macc, 1'b0};\n\n    // exponent differences (unsigned 7-bit; true diff is always in [0,127])\n    logic [NL*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] diffv;\n    generate\n        genvar gn;\n        for (gn = 0; gn < NL; gn++) begin : g_diff\n            assign diffv[gn*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                pipe2_max - pipe2_items[gn*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // align shift + sign fold\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] folded;\n    generate\n        genvar go;\n        for (go = 0; go < NL; go++) begin : g_sh\n            wire [(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] dv = diffv[go*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n            wire big = dv[6] | dv[5] | dv[4];\n            wire [ASH_W-1:0] sa = big ? ALIGN_WIDTH[ASH_W-1:0] : dv[ASH_W-1:0];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pv = prod14[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh = pv >> sa;\n            assign folded[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = sh ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[go]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // correction row: -k*2^14 + k (mod 2^20)\n    wire [5:0] nc6 = (~{1'b0, pipe2_k}) + 6'd1;\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] corr = {nc6, {(ROW_W-6-CARRY_W){1'b0}}, pipe2_k};\n\n    logic [(NL+1)*ROW_W-1:0] csa_rows;\n    generate\n        genvar gp;\n        for (gp = 0; gp < NL; gp++) begin : g_rows\n            assign csa_rows[gp*ROW_W +: ROW_W] = {{(ROW_W-ALIGN_WIDTH){1'b0}}, folded[gp*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign csa_rows[NL*ROW_W +: ROW_W] = corr;\n\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] csa_s, csa_c;\n    csa_reduce #(.N(NL+1), .W(ROW_W)) u_csa (.rows(csa_rows), .s_o(csa_s), .c_o(csa_c));\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [ROW_W-1:0] pipe3_s, pipe3_c;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_max;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_s   <= csa_s;\n        pipe3_c   <= csa_c;\n        pipe3_max <= pipe2_max;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Stage 4: final carry-propagate add\n    // ********************\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] sum_full = pipe3_s + pipe3_c;\n\n    logic [ROW_W-1:0] pipe4_sum;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_max;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_sum <= sum_full;\n        pipe4_max <= pipe3_max;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Stage 5: normalize\n    // ********************\n    wire p4sign = pipe4_sum[ROW_W-1];\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] negv = ~pipe4_sum + 20'd1;\n    wire [SUM_W-1:0] sum_c = p4sign ? negv[SUM_W-1:0] : pipe4_sum[SUM_W-1:0];\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adj;\n    wire [SUM_W-1:0] sum_norm;\n    mantissa_norm #(\n        .WIDTH(SUM_W),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_W+2)\n    ) u_mantissa_norm (\n        .operand_i(sum_c),\n        .exp_adjust(exp_adj),\n        .result_o(sum_norm)\n    );\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] fre = pipe4_max + exp_adj;\n    wire sticky = |sum_norm[SUM_W-MANT_WIDTH_O-3-:6];\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] fman = {sum_norm[SUM_W-1:SUM_W-MANT_WIDTH_O-2], sticky};\n\n    logic pipe5_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_re;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_mant;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe5_sign <= p4sign;\n        pipe5_re   <= fre;\n        pipe5_mant <= fman;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Stage 6 (output): encode\n    // ********************\n    wire [n_o-1:0] res;\n    posit_encoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)\n    ) u_posit_encoder (\n        .sign_i(pipe5_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(pipe5_re),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_mant),\n        .result_o(res)\n    );\n    assign result_o = res;\n\nendmodule\n")

[ASSISTANT]
The `eval_1/` directory isn't accessible via `ls`, so I'll optimize by reasoning. My restructure gave 866 area (vs 1063 baseline) but the critical path is stage 3 (align+compress ≈ 950 ps). The big lever is **rebalancing the pipeline**: move the multiplier, maxtree and exponent-diff into stage 2, leaving only shift+fold+compress in stage 3, CPA alone in stage 4, norm in stage 5, encoder after pipe5.
  -> create_file(content="// pdpu_top_pipelined -- 5-stage pipelined 16-lane posit dot-product-accumulate.\n// Bit-exact to the combinational reference pdpu_dot16 (ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation,\n// NaR handling identical). Pipeline rebalanced for a short critical path:\n//   R1 in->pipe1 : decode (33x) + exponent add + sign popcount\n//   R2 pipe1->2  : 16x (4x4 mantissa mul) + max tree (17x7) + exponent diff\n//   R3 pipe2->3  : align shift + sign fold + correction row + CSA compress\n//   R4 pipe3->4  : final carry-propagate add\n//   R5 pipe4->5  : abs + normalize + final exponent + mantissa/sticky\n//   R6 pipe5->out: posit encode\n// Per-lane two's-complement adders are replaced by XOR-folding plus a single\n// popcount correction row folded into the compressor tree (exact in Z/(2^20)).\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);\n    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;\n    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] sel_nodes;\n    logic [(2**NumLevels)*NumLevels-1:0] index_nodes;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n    always_comb begin : flip_vector\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n        in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n      end\n    end\n    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n                index_lut[(k * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 == WIDTH - 1) begin : g_base\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 > WIDTH - 1) begin : g_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =\n              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n  end : gen_lzc\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule barrel_shifter #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;\n    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        if(MODE==1'b0) begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]>>(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]>>1 : temp_results;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] operand_value;\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign operand_value = sign ? (~operand_i[n-2:0]+1) : operand_i[n-2:0];\n\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(operand_i),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else if(leading_zero_count <= DECIMAL_POINT - 1) begin\n            exp_adjust = DECIMAL_POINT - leading_zero_count - 1;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = -$signed(leading_zero_count - DECIMAL_POINT + 1);\n        end\n    end\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? {1'b1,~value_after_round+1} : {1'b0,value_after_round};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\n// balanced signed maximum tree\nmodule maxtree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 7\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] items,\n    output logic signed [W-1:0] mx\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign mx = $signed(items[0 +: W]);\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            wire signed [W-1:0] a = $signed(items[0 +: W]);\n            wire signed [W-1:0] b = $signed(items[W +: W]);\n            assign mx = (a > b) ? a : b;\n        end else begin : split\n            wire signed [W-1:0] ma, mb;\n            maxtree #(.N(NA), .W(W)) ua (.items(items[0 +: NA*W]), .mx(ma));\n            maxtree #(.N(NB), .W(W)) ub (.items(items[NA*W +: NB*W]), .mx(mb));\n            assign mx = (ma > mb) ? ma : mb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// balanced popcount tree\nmodule popcount #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17\n)(\n    input  logic [N-1:0] v,\n    output logic [pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1)-1:0] cnt\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign cnt = v[0];\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            assign cnt = v[0] + v[1];\n        end else begin : split\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NA+1)-1:0] ca;\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NB+1)-1:0] cb;\n            popcount #(.N(NA)) ua (.v(v[0 +: NA]), .cnt(ca));\n            popcount #(.N(NB)) ub (.v(v[NA +: NB]), .cnt(cb));\n            assign cnt = ca + cb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// greedy Wallace reduction of N rows (width W) down to two rows using 3:2\n// compressors (full adders, no serial carry chains). Depth = 6 for N=18.\nmodule csa_reduce #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 18,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 20\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] rows,\n    output logic [W-1:0] s_o,\n    output logic [W-1:0] c_o\n);\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : b1\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = '0;\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : b2\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = rows[W +: W];\n        end else begin : rec\n            localparam int unsigned M = N/3;\n            localparam int unsigned REM = N - 3*M;\n            localparam int unsigned NN = 2*M + REM;\n            wire [M*W-1:0] ts, tc;\n            genvar j;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : trip\n                wire [W-1:0] ra = rows[(3*j+0)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rb = rows[(3*j+1)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rc = rows[(3*j+2)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] ab = ra & rb;\n                wire [W-1:0] aob = ra | rb;\n                assign ts[j*W +: W] = ra ^ rb ^ rc;\n                assign tc[j*W +: W] = {((ab[W-2:0]) | (rc[W-2:0] & aob[W-2:0])), 1'b0};\n            end\n            wire [NN*W-1:0] nxt;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : pack\n                assign nxt[(2*j)*W +: W] = ts[j*W +: W];\n                assign nxt[(2*j+1)*W +: W] = tc[j*W +: W];\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < REM; j++) begin : pass\n                assign nxt[(2*M+j)*W +: W] = rows[(3*M+j)*W +: W];\n            end\n            csa_reduce #(.N(NN), .W(W)) sub (.rows(nxt), .s_o(s_o), .c_o(c_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i - es_i - 3;                  // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o - es_o - 3;                  // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1, EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MUL_W = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned CARRY_W = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);                 // 5\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_W = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;                  // 19\n    localparam int unsigned ASH_W = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);         // 4\n    localparam int unsigned NL = N + 1;                                     // 17 items/rows\n    localparam int unsigned ROW_W = SUM_W + 1;                              // 20\n\n    // ********************\n    // Region 1: decode + exponent add + popcount  (in -> pipe1)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    wire sign_acc;\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_acc;\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o)) u_dec_acc (\n        .operand_i(acc), .sign_o(sign_acc), .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc), .mant_norm_o(mant_acc)\n    );\n\n    generate\n        genvar gi;\n        for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi++) begin : g_dec\n            wire sg_a, sg_b;\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] re_a, re_b;\n            wire [MANT_WIDTH_I:0] mn_a, mn_b;\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_a),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_a), .mant_norm_o(mn_a)\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_b),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_b), .mant_norm_o(mn_b)\n            );\n            assign signs_a[gi] = sg_a;\n            assign signs_b[gi] = sg_b;\n            assign rg_exp_a[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_a;\n            assign rg_exp_b[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_b;\n            assign mants_a[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_a;\n            assign mants_b[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_b;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    wire [N-1:0] signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    wire [N:0] signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar gj;\n        for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj++) begin : g_eadd\n            assign rg_exp_c[gj*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                $signed(rg_exp_a[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]) +\n                $signed(rg_exp_b[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)]);\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    wire [CARRY_W-1:0] kpop;\n    popcount #(.N(NL)) u_pop (.v(signs), .cnt(kpop));\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_exp;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_ma, pipe1_mb;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_macc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_eacc;\n    logic [CARRY_W-1:0] pipe1_k;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs <= signs;\n        pipe1_exp   <= rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_ma    <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mb    <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_macc  <= mant_acc;\n        pipe1_eacc  <= rg_exp_acc;\n        pipe1_k     <= kpop;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Region 2: multiply + max + diff  (pipe1 -> pipe2)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] prod;\n    generate\n        genvar gk;\n        for (gk = 0; gk < N; gk++) begin : g_mul\n            assign prod[gk*MUL_W +: MUL_W] =\n                pipe1_ma[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe1_mb[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [NL*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] items;\n    generate\n        genvar gl;\n        for (gl = 0; gl < N; gl++) begin : g_it\n            assign items[gl*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_exp[gl*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign items[N*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_eacc;\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] emax;\n    maxtree #(.N(NL), .W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_maxtree (.items(items), .mx(emax));\n\n    // exponent differences (unsigned 7-bit; true diff is always in [0,127])\n    logic [NL*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] diffv;\n    logic [NL*ASH_W-1:0] sav;\n    generate\n        genvar gn;\n        for (gn = 0; gn < NL; gn++) begin : g_diff\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] dv = emax - items[gn*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n            assign diffv[gn*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = dv;\n            assign sav[gn*ASH_W +: ASH_W] = (|dv[EXP_WIDTH:ASH_W]) ? ALIGN_WIDTH[ASH_W-1:0] : dv[ASH_W-1:0];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] pipe2_prod;\n    logic [NL*ASH_W-1:0] pipe2_sa;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_max;\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic [CARRY_W-1:0] pipe2_k;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_macc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_prod  <= prod;\n        pipe2_sa    <= sav;\n        pipe2_max   <= emax;\n        pipe2_signs <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_k     <= pipe1_k;\n        pipe2_macc  <= pipe1_macc;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Region 3: align + fold + compress  (pipe2 -> pipe3)\n    // ********************\n    // pre-alignment products, left justified in ALIGN_WIDTH bits\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] prod14;\n    generate\n        genvar gm;\n        for (gm = 0; gm < N; gm++) begin : g_p14\n            assign prod14[gm*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n                {pipe2_prod[gm*MUL_W +: MUL_W], {(ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_W){1'b0}}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign prod14[N*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = {1'b0, pipe2_macc, 1'b0};\n\n    // align shift + sign fold\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] folded;\n    generate\n        genvar go;\n        for (go = 0; go < NL; go++) begin : g_sh\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pv = prod14[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh = pv >> pipe2_sa[go*ASH_W +: ASH_W];\n            assign folded[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = sh ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[go]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // correction row: -k*2^14 + k (mod 2^20)\n    wire [5:0] nc6 = (~{1'b0, pipe2_k}) + 6'd1;\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] corr = {nc6, {(ROW_W-6-CARRY_W){1'b0}}, pipe2_k};\n\n    logic [(NL+1)*ROW_W-1:0] csa_rows;\n    generate\n        genvar gp;\n        for (gp = 0; gp < NL; gp++) begin : g_rows\n            assign csa_rows[gp*ROW_W +: ROW_W] = {{(ROW_W-ALIGN_WIDTH){1'b0}}, folded[gp*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign csa_rows[NL*ROW_W +: ROW_W] = corr;\n\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] csa_s, csa_c;\n    csa_reduce #(.N(NL+1), .W(ROW_W)) u_csa (.rows(csa_rows), .s_o(csa_s), .c_o(csa_c));\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [ROW_W-1:0] pipe3_s, pipe3_c;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_max;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_s   <= csa_s;\n        pipe3_c   <= csa_c;\n        pipe3_max <= pipe2_max;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Region 4: final carry-propagate add  (pipe3 -> pipe4)\n    // ********************\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] sum_full = pipe3_s + pipe3_c;\n\n    logic [ROW_W-1:0] pipe4_sum;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_max;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_sum <= sum_full;\n        pipe4_max <= pipe3_max;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Region 5: abs + normalize  (pipe4 -> pipe5)\n    // ********************\n    wire p4sign = pipe4_sum[ROW_W-1];\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] negv = ~pipe4_sum + 20'd1;\n    wire [SUM_W-1:0] sum_c = p4sign ? negv[SUM_W-1:0] : pipe4_sum[SUM_W-1:0];\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adj;\n    wire [SUM_W-1:0] sum_norm;\n    mantissa_norm #(\n        .WIDTH(SUM_W),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_W+2)\n    ) u_mantissa_norm (\n        .operand_i(sum_c),\n        .exp_adjust(exp_adj),\n        .result_o(sum_norm)\n    );\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] fre = pipe4_max + exp_adj;\n    wire sticky = |sum_norm[SUM_W-MANT_WIDTH_O-3-:6];\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] fman = {sum_norm[SUM_W-1:SUM_W-MANT_WIDTH_O-2], sticky};\n\n    logic pipe5_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_re;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_mant;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe5_sign <= p4sign;\n        pipe5_re   <= fre;\n        pipe5_mant <= fman;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // Region 6: encode  (pipe5 -> out)\n    // ********************\n    wire [n_o-1:0] res;\n    posit_encoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)\n    ) u_posit_encoder (\n        .sign_i(pipe5_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(pipe5_re),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_mant),\n        .result_o(res)\n    );\n    assign result_o = res;\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design2.v')

[ASSISTANT]
design2 v2 improved to 808k but stage 3 (diff+shift+compress) is still 917 ps. My calibrated estimates: subtract ≈270ps, clamp ≈110, shift ≈240, compress ≈200, clk→Q ≈136. Plan: move the exponent-diff into region 2 with a **Kogge-Stone prefix subtractor**, drop the clamp (use a `big` flag + mask), use a prefix CPA, and merge the encoder's rounding-add with its output negation into one prefix add.
  -> create_file(content="// pdpu_top_pipelined -- 5-stage pipelined 16-lane posit dot-product-accumulate.\n// Bit-exact to the combinational reference pdpu_dot16 (ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation,\n// NaR handling identical). Balanced pipeline + parallel-prefix arithmetic:\n//   R1 in->pipe1 : decode (33x) + product-exponent add\n//   R2 pipe1->2  : 16x (4x4 mul) || max tree (17x7) || popcount || prefix subtract+big flag\n//   R3 pipe2->3  : align shift + sign fold + corr row + CSA compress (18 rows -> 2)\n//   R4 pipe3->4  : prefix carry-propagate add\n//   R5 pipe4->5  : abs + normalize + final exponent + mantissa/sticky\n//   R6 pipe5->out: posit encode (rounding add merged with output negation)\n// Per-lane two's-complement adders are replaced by XOR-folding plus a single\n// popcount correction row folded into the compressor tree (exact in Z/(2^20)).\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);\n    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;\n    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] sel_nodes;\n    logic [(2**NumLevels)*NumLevels-1:0] index_nodes;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n    always_comb begin : flip_vector\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n        in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n      end\n    end\n    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n                index_lut[(k * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 == WIDTH - 1) begin : g_base\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 > WIDTH - 1) begin : g_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =\n              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n  end : gen_lzc\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule barrel_shifter #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;\n    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        if(MODE==1'b0) begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]<<(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]<<1 : temp_results;\n        end\n        else begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(operand_i),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n    // exp_adjust = lzc_zeroes ? 0 : (DECIMAL_POINT-1 - lz)   (both reference branches agree)\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] lz_ext;\n    assign lz_ext = leading_zero_count;\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = $signed(DECIMAL_POINT-1) - $signed({{(EXP_WIDTH-LZC_WIDTH+1){1'b0}}, leading_zero_count});\n        end\n    end\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n\n    // merged: value_before_round ^ {(n-1){sign_i}};\n    ks_add #(\n        .W(n-1)\n    ) u_round_add (\n        .a(add_a),\n        .b({{(n-2){1'b0}}, round_value ^ sign_i}),\n        .cin(1'b0),\n        .s(add_s)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = {sign_i, add_s};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\n// Kogge-Stone parallel-prefix adder: s = a + b + cin (mod 2^W)\nmodule ks_add #(\n    parameter int unsigned W = 8\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [W-1:0] b,\n    input  logic         cin,\n    output logic [W-1:0] s\n);\n    localparam int unsigned L = pdpu_pkg::clog2(W);\n    logic [(L+1)*W-1:0] G, P;\n    genvar gi, gl;\n    generate\n        for (gi = 0; gi < W; gi++) begin : g_init\n            assign G[gi] = a[gi] & b[gi];\n            assign P[gi] = a[gi] ^ b[gi];\n        end\n        if (W == 1) begin : g_w1\n            assign s[0] = a[0] ^ b[0] ^ cin;\n        end else begin : g_main\n            assign G[0] = (a[0] & b[0]) | ((a[0] ^ b[0]) & cin);\n            for (gl = 1; gl <= L; gl++) begin : g_lvl\n                localparam int unsigned SP = 1 << (gl-1);\n                for (gi = 0; gi < W; gi++) begin : g_bit\n                    if (gi < SP) begin : g_pass\n                        assign G[gl*W + gi] = G[(gl-1)*W + gi];\n                        assign P[gl*W + gi] = P[(gl-1)*W + gi];\n                    end else begin : g_comb\n                        assign G[gl*W + gi] = G[(gl-1)*W + gi] | (P[(gl-1)*W + gi] & G[(gl-1)*W + gi - SP]);\n                        assign P[gl*W + gi] = P[(gl-1)*W + gi] & P[(gl-1)*W + gi - SP];\n                    end\n                end\n            end\n            assign s = P[W-1:0] ^ {G[L*W + W-2 -: W-1], cin};\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// balanced signed maximum tree\nmodule maxtree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 7\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] items,\n    output logic signed [W-1:0] mx\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign mx = $signed(items[0 +: W]);\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            wire signed [W-1:0] a = $signed(items[0 +: W]);\n            wire signed [W-1:0] b = $signed(items[W +: W]);\n            assign mx = (a > b) ? a : b;\n        end else begin : split\n            wire signed [W-1:0] ma, mb;\n            maxtree #(.N(NA), .W(W)) ua (.items(items[0 +: NA*W]), .mx(ma));\n            maxtree #(.N(NB), .W(W)) ub (.items(items[NA*W +: NB*W]), .mx(mb));\n            assign mx = (ma > mb) ? ma : mb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// balanced popcount tree\nmodule popcount #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17\n)(\n    input  logic [N-1:0] v,\n    output logic [pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1)-1:0] cnt\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign cnt = v[0];\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            assign cnt = v[0] + v[1];\n        end else begin : split\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NA+1)-1:0] ca;\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NB+1)-1:0] cb;\n            popcount #(.N(NA)) ua (.v(v[0 +: NA]), .cnt(ca));\n            popcount #(.N(NB)) ub (.v(v[NA +: NB]), .cnt(cb));\n            assign cnt = ca + cb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// greedy Wallace reduction of N rows (width W) down to two rows using 3:2\n// compressors (full adders, no serial carry chains). Depth = 6 for N=18.\nmodule csa_reduce #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 18,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 20\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] rows,\n    output logic [W-1:0] s_o,\n    output logic [W-1:0] c_o\n);\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : b1\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = '0;\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : b2\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = rows[W +: W];\n        end else begin : rec\n            localparam int unsigned M = N/3;\n            localparam int unsigned REM = N - 3*M;\n            localparam int unsigned NN = 2*M + REM;\n            wire [M*W-1:0] ts, tc;\n            genvar j;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : trip\n                wire [W-1:0] ra = rows[(3*j+0)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rb = rows[(3*j+1)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rc = rows[(3*j+2)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] ab = ra & rb;\n                wire [W-1:0] aob = ra | rb;\n                assign ts[j*W +: W] = ra ^ rb ^ rc;\n                assign tc[j*W +: W] = {((ab[W-2:0]) | (rc[W-2:0] & aob[W-2:0])), 1'b0};\n            end\n            wire [NN*W-1:0] .c_o(c_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input
