Balanced pipeline + parallel-prefix arithmetic:\n//   R1 in->pipe1 : decode (33x) + product-exponent add\n//   R2 pipe1->2  : 16x (4x4 mul) || max tree (17x7) || popcount || prefix subtract+big flag\n//   R3 pipe2->3  : align shift + sign fold + corr row + CSA compress (18 rows -> 2)\n//   R4 pipe3->4  : prefix carry-propagate add\n//   R5 pipe4->5  : abs + normalize + final exponent + mantissa/sticky\n//   R6 pipe5->out: posit encode (rounding add merged with output negation)\n// Per-lane two's-complement adders are replaced by XOR-folding plus a single\n// popcount correction row folded into the compressor tree (exact in Z/(2^20)).\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit          MODE  = 1'b0,\n  parameter int unsigned CNT_WIDTH = cf_math_pkg::idx_width(WIDTH)\n) (\n  input  logic [WIDTH-1:0]     in_i,\n  output logic [CNT_WIDTH-1:0] cnt_o,\n  output logic                 empty_o\n);\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH);\n    logic [WIDTH*NumLevels-1:0] index_lut;\n    logic [2**NumLevels-1:0] sel_nodes;\n    logic [(2**NumLevels)*NumLevels-1:0] index_nodes;\n    logic [WIDTH-1:0] in_tmp;\n    always_comb begin : flip_vector\n      for (int unsigned i = 0; i < WIDTH; i++) begin\n        in_tmp[i] = (MODE) ? in_i[WIDTH-1-i] : in_i[i];\n      end\n    end\n    for (genvar j = 0; $unsigned(j) < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n                index_lut[(k * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 == WIDTH - 1) begin : g_base\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 > WIDTH - 1) begin : g_out_of_range\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = 1'b0;\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = '0;\n          end\n        end\n      end else begin : g_not_last_level\n        for (genvar l = 0; l < 2 ** level; l++) begin : g_level\n          assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + l] =\n              sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] | sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1];\n          assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + l)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (sel_nodes[2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2] == 1'b1)\n            ? index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] :\n              index_nodes[(2 ** (level + 1) - 1 + l * 2 + 1)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n        end\n      end\n    end\n    assign cnt_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? index_nodes[(0)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] : {(cf_math_pkg::clog(WIDTH)) {1'b0}};\n    assign empty_o = NumLevels > $unsigned(0) ? ~sel_nodes[0] : ~(|in_i);\n  end : gen_lzc\nendmodule : lzc\n\nmodule barrel_shifter #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter bit MODE = 1'b0\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    input logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0][WIDTH-1:0] temp_results;\n    assign temp_results[SHIFT_WIDTH-1] = operand_i;\n    generate\n        genvar i;\n        if(MODE==1'b0) begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]<<(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]<<1 : temp_results;\n        end\n        else begin\n            for(i=SHIFT_WIDTH-1;i>0;i--) begin\n                assign temp_results[i-1] = shift_amount[i] ? temp_results[i]>>(2**i) : temp_results[i];\n            end\n            assign result_o = shift_amount[0] ? temp_results[0]>>1 : temp_results;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// inclusive OR-prefix scan: y[i] = |x[i:0]\nmodule or_scan #(\n    parameter int unsigned M = 7\n)(\n    input  logic [M-1:0] x,\n    output logic [M-1:0] y\n);\n    localparam int unsigned L = pdpu_pkg::clog2(M);\n    logic [(L+1)*M-1:0] t;\n    genvar gi, gl;\n    generate\n        for (gi = 0; gi < M; gi++) begin : g_init\n            assign t[gi] = x[gi];\n        end\n        for (gl = 1; gl <= L; gl++) begin : g_lvl\n            localparam int unsigned SP = 1 << (gl-1);\n            for (gi = 0; gi < M; gi++) begin : g_bit\n                if (gi < SP) begin : g_pass\n                    assign t[gl*M + gi] = t[(gl-1)*M + gi];\n                end else begin : g_comb\n                    assign t[gl*M + gi] = t[(gl-1)*M + gi] | t[(gl-1)*M + gi - SP];\n                end\n            end\n        end\n        assign y = t[L*M +: M];\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// Kogge-Stone parallel-prefix adder: s = a + b + cin (mod 2^W)\nmodule ks_add #(\n    parameter int unsigned W = 8\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [W-1:0] b,\n    input  logic         cin,\n    output logic [W-1:0] s\n);\n    localparam int unsigned L = pdpu_pkg::clog2(W);\n    logic [(L+1)*W-1:0] G, P;\n    genvar gi, gl;\n    generate\n        for (gi = 0; gi < W; gi++) begin : g_init\n            if (gi == 0) begin : g_cin\n                assign G[0] = (a[0] & b[0]) | ((a[0] ^ b[0]) & cin);\n            end else begin : g_std\n                assign G[gi] = a[gi] & b[gi];\n            end\n            assign P[gi] = a[gi] ^ b[gi];\n        end\n        if (W == 1) begin : g_w1\n            assign s[0] = a[0] ^ b[0] ^ cin;\n        end else begin : g_main\n            for (gl = 1; gl <= L; gl++) begin : g_lvl\n                localparam int unsigned SP = 1 << (gl-1);\n                for (gi = 0; gi < W; gi++) begin : g_bit\n                    if (gi < SP) begin : g_pass\n                        assign G[gl*W + gi] = G[(gl-1)*W + gi];\n                        assign P[gl*W + gi] = P[(gl-1)*W + gi];\n                    end else begin : g_comb\n                        assign G[gl*W + gi] = G[(gl-1)*W + gi] | (P[(gl-1)*W + gi] & G[(gl-1)*W + gi - SP]);\n                        assign P[gl*W + gi] = P[(gl-1)*W + gi] & P[(gl-1)*W + gi - SP];\n                    end\n                end\n            end\n            // s[i] = P0[i] ^ carry-into-bit-i; carry into bit 0 is cin\n            assign s = P[W-1:0] ^ {G[L*W + W-2 -: W-1], cin};\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] z, operand_value;\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign z = operand_i[n-2:0];\n    // (-z)[i] = z[i] ^ |z[i-1:0]| (i>=1), (-z)[0] = z[0]\n    logic [n-2:0] S;\n    or_scan #(.M(n-1)) u_scan (.x(z), .y(S));\n    assign operand_value = z ^ ({(n-1){sign}} & {S[n-3:0], 1'b0});\n\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule mantissa_norm #(\n    parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = 3,\n    parameter int unsigned DECIMAL_POINT = 3\n)(\n    input logic [WIDTH-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adjust,\n    output logic [WIDTH-1:0] result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(WIDTH);\n    logic [LZC_WIDTH-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(operand_i),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n    // exp_adjust = lzc_zeroes ? 0 : (DECIMAL_POINT-1 - lz)   (both reference branches agree)\n    always_comb begin\n        if(lzc_zeroes) begin\n            exp_adjust = '0;\n        end\n        else begin\n            exp_adjust = $signed(DECIMAL_POINT-1) - $signed({{(EXP_WIDTH-LZC_WIDTH+1){1'b0}}, leading_zero_count});\n        end\n    end\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(WIDTH),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_i),\n        .shift_amount(leading_zero_count),\n        .result_o(result_o)\n    );\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n\n    // merged: normal_result[n-2:0] = sign_i ? -(vbr+rv) : (vbr+rv)\n    //       = (vbr ^ {sign}) + (rv ^ sign)          (exact in Z/(2^(n-1)))\n    logic [n-2:0] add_a, add_s;\n    assign add_a = value_before_round ^ {(n-1){sign_i}};\n    ks_add #(\n        .W(n-1)\n    ) u_round_add (\n        .a(add_a),\n        .b({{(n-2){1'b0}}, round_value ^ sign_i}),\n        .cin(1'b0),\n        .s(add_s)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = {sign_i, add_s};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\n// balanced signed maximum tree\nmodule maxtree #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 7\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] items,\n    output logic signed [W-1:0] mx\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign mx = $signed(items[0 +: W]);\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            wire signed [W-1:0] a = $signed(items[0 +: W]);\n            wire signed [W-1:0] b = $signed(items[W +: W]);\n            assign mx = (a > b) ? a : b;\n        end else begin : split\n            wire signed [W-1:0] ma, mb;\n            maxtree #(.N(NA), .W(W)) ua (.items(items[0 +: NA*W]), .mx(ma));\n            maxtree #(.N(NB), .W(W)) ub (.items(items[NA*W +: NB*W]), .mx(mb));\n            assign mx = (ma > mb) ? ma : mb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// balanced popcount tree\nmodule popcount #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17\n)(\n    input  logic [N-1:0] v,\n    output logic [pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1)-1:0] cnt\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign cnt = v[0];\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            assign cnt = v[0] + v[1];\n        end else begin : split\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NA+1)-1:0] ca;\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NB+1)-1:0] cb;\n            popcount #(.N(NA)) ua (.v(v[0 +: NA]), .cnt(ca));\n            popcount #(.N(NB)) ub (.v(v[NA +: NB]), .cnt(cb));\n            assign cnt = ca + cb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// greedy Wallace reduction of N rows (width W) down to two rows using 3:2\n// compressors (full adders, no serial carry chains). Depth = 6 for N=18.\nmodule csa_reduce #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 18,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 20\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] rows,\n    output logic [W-1:0] s_o,\n    output logic [W-1:0] c_o\n);\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : b1\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = '0;\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : b2\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = rows[W +: W];\n        end else begin : rec\n            localparam int unsigned M = N/3;\n            localparam int unsigned REM = N - 3*M;\n            localparam int unsigned NN = 2*M + REM;\n            wire [M*W-1:0] ts, tc;\n            genvar j;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : trip\n                wire [W-1:0] ra = rows[(3*j+0)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rb = rows[(3*j+1)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rc = rows[(3*j+2)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] ab = ra & rb;\n                wire [W-1:0] aob = ra | rb;\n                assign ts[j*W +: W] = ra ^ rb ^ rc;\n                assign tc[j*W +: W] = {((ab[W-2:0]) | (rc[W-2:0] & aob[W-2:0])), 1'b0};\n            end\n            wire [NN*W-1:0] nxt;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : pack\n                assign nxt[(2*j)*W +: W] = ts[j*W +: W];\n                assign nxt[(2*j+1)*W +: W] = tc[j*W +: W];\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < REM; j++) begin : pass\n                assign nxt[(2*M+j)*W +: W] = rows[(3*M+j)*W +: W];\n            end\n            csa_reduce #(.N(NN), .W(W)) sub (.rows(nxt), .s_o(s_o), .c_o(c_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i - es_i - 3;                  // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o - es_o - 3;                  // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1, EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MUL_W = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned CARRY_W = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);                 // 5\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_W = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;                  // 19\n    localparam int unsigned ASH_W = pdpu_pkg::clog2(ALIGN_WIDTH+1);         // 4\n    localparam int unsigned NL = N + 1;                                     // 17\n    localparam int unsigned ROW_W = SUM_W + 1;                              // 20\n\n    // ********************\n    // R1: decode + product exponent add   (in -> pipe1)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    wire sign_acc;\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_acc;\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o)) u_dec_acc (\n        .operand_i(acc), .sign_o(sign_acc), .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc), .mant_norm_o(mant_acc)\n    );\n\n    generate\n        genvar gi;\n        for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi++) begin : g_dec\n            wire sg_a, sg_b;\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] re_a, re_b;\n            wire [MANT_WIDTH_I:0] mn_a, mn_b;\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_a),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_a), .mant_norm_o(mn_a)\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_b),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_b), .mant_norm_o(mn_b)\n            );\n            assign signs_a[gi] = sg_a;\n            assign signs_b[gi] = sg_b;\n            assign rg_exp_a[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_a;\n            assign rg_exp_b[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_b;\n            assign mants_a[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_a;\n            assign mants_b[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_b;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    wire [N-1:0] signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    wire [N:0] signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar gj;\n        for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj++) begin : g_eadd\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] ea = rg_exp_a[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)];\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] eb = rg_exp_b[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)];\n            ks_add #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_eadd (.a(ea), .b(eb), .cin(1'b0), .s(rg_exp_c[gj*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]));\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_exp;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_ma, pipe1_mb;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_macc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_eacc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs <= signs;\n        pipe1_exp   <= rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_ma    <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mb    <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_macc  <= mant_acc;\n        pipe1_eacc  <= rg_exp_acc;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R2: multiply || max tree || popcount || exponent diff   (pipe1 -> pipe2)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] prod;\n    generate\n        genvar gk;\n        for (gk = 0; gk < N; gk++) begin : g_mul\n            assign prod[gk*MUL_W +: MUL_W] =\n                pipe1_ma[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe1_mb[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [NL*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] items;\n    generate\n        genvar gl;\n        for (gl = 0; gl < N; gl++) begin : g_it\n            assign items[gl*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_exp[gl*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign items[N*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_eacc;\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] emax;\n    maxtree #(.N(NL), .W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_maxtree (.items(items), .mx(emax));\n\n    wire [CARRY_W-1:0] kpop;\n    popcount #(.N(NL)) u_pop (.v(pipe1_signs), .cnt(kpop));\n\n    // exponent differences: dv = emax - item (exact, always in [0,127])\n    logic [NL*ASH_W-1:0] sav;\n    logic [NL-1:0] bigv;\n    generate\n        genvar gn;\n        for (gn = 0; gn < NL; gn++) begin : g_diff\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] dv;\n            ks_add #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_sub (\n                .a(emax[EXP_WIDTH:0]),\n                .b(~items[gn*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .cin(1'b1),\n                .s(dv)\n            );\n            // diff >= 16: shifting by dv[3:0] would be wrong -> zero the row instead\n            // (diff = 14,15 already shift all bits out naturally)\n            assign bigv[gn] = dv[6] | dv[5] | dv[4];\n            assign sav[gn*ASH_W +: ASH_W] = dv[ASH_W-1:0];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] pipe2_prod;\n    logic [NL*ASH_W-1:0] pipe2_sa;\n    logic [NL-1:0] pipe2_big;\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic [CARRY_W-1:0] pipe2_k;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_macc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_prod  <= prod;\n        pipe2_sa    <= sav;\n        pipe2_big   <= bigv;\n        pipe2_signs <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_k     <= kpop;\n        pipe2_macc  <= pipe1_macc;\n        pipe2_max   <= emax;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R3: align + fold + compress   (pipe2 -> pipe3)\n    // ********************\n    // pre-alignment products, left justified in ALIGN_WIDTH bits\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] prod14;\n    generate\n        genvar gm;\n        for (gm = 0; gm < N; gm++) begin : g_p14\n            assign prod14[gm*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n                {pipe2_prod[gm*MUL_W +: MUL_W], {(ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_W){1'b0}}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign prod14[N*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = {1'b0, pipe2_macc, 1'b0};\n\n    // align shift + big-row zeroing + sign fold\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] folded;\n    generate\n        genvar go;\n        for (go = 0; go < NL; go++) begin : g_sh\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pv = prod14[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh = pv >> pipe2_sa[go*ASH_W +: ASH_W];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] shz = sh & ~{ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_big[go]}};\n            assign folded[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = shz ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[go]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // correction row: -k*2^ALIGN_WIDTH + k (mod 2^ROW_W)\n    wire [5:0] nc6 = -{1'b0, pipe2_k};\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] corr = {nc6, {(ROW_W-6-CARRY_W){1'b0}}, pipe2_k};\n\n    logic [(NL+1)*ROW_W-1:0] csa_rows;\n    generate\n        genvar gp;\n        for (gp = 0; gp < NL; gp++) begin : g_rows\n            assign csa_rows[gp*ROW_W +: ROW_W] = {{(ROW_W-ALIGN_WIDTH){1'b0}}, folded[gp*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign csa_rows[NL*ROW_W +: ROW_W] = corr;\n\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] csa_s, csa_c;\n    csa_reduce #(.N(NL+1), .W(ROW_W)) u_csa (.rows(csa_rows), .s_o(csa_s), .c_o(csa_c));\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [ROW_W-1:0] pipe3_s, pipe3_c;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_max;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_s   <= csa_s;\n        pipe3_c   <= csa_c;\n        pipe3_max <= pipe2_max;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R4: final carry-propagate add   (pipe3 -> pipe4)\n    // ********************\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] sum_full;\n    ks_add #(.W(ROW_W)) u_cpa (.a(pipe3_s), .b(pipe3_c), .cin(1'b0), .s(sum_full));\n\n    logic [ROW_W-1:0] pipe4_sum;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_max;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_sum <= sum_full;\n        pipe4_max <= pipe3_max;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R5: abs + normalize   (pipe4 -> pipe5)\n    // ********************\n    wire p4sign = pipe4_sum[ROW_W-1];\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] negv;\n    ks_add #(.W(ROW_W)) u_neg (.a(~pipe4_sum), .b('0), .cin(1'b1), .s(negv));\n    wire [SUM_W-1:0] sum_c = p4sign ? negv[SUM_W-1:0] : pipe4_sum[SUM_W-1:0];\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] exp_adj;\n    wire [SUM_W-1:0] sum_norm;\n    mantissa_norm #(\n        .WIDTH(SUM_W),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .DECIMAL_POINT(CARRY_W+2)\n    ) u_mantissa_norm (\n        .operand_i(sum_c),\n        .exp_adjust(exp_adj),\n        .result_o(sum_norm)\n    );\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] fre = pipe4_max + exp_adj;\n    wire sticky = |sum_norm[SUM_W-MANT_WIDTH_O-3-:6];\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] fman = {sum_norm[SUM_W-1:SUM_W-MANT_WIDTH_O-2], sticky};\n\n    logic pipe5_sign;\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_re;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_mant;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe5_sign <= p4sign;\n        pipe5_re   <= fre;\n        pipe5_mant <= fman;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R6: encode   (pipe5 -> out)\n    // ********************\n    wire [n_o-1:0] res;\n    posit_encoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)\n    ) u_posit_encoder (\n        .sign_i(pipe5_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(pipe5_re),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_mant),\n        .result_o(res)\n    );\n    assign result_o = res;\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design4.v')

                     // 20\n\n    // ********************\n    // R1: decode + product exponent add   (in -> pipe1)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    wire sign_acc;\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_acc;\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o)) u_dec_acc (\n        .operand_i(acc), .sign_o(sign_acc), .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc), .mant_norm_o(mant_acc)\n    );\n\n    generate\n        genvar gi;\n        for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi++) begin : g_dec\n            wire sg_a, sg_b;\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] re_a, re_b;\n            wire [MANT_WIDTH_I:0] mn_a, mn_b;\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), endgenerate\n\n    wire [N-1:0] signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    wire [N:0] signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar gj;\n        for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj++) begin : g_eadd\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] ea = rg_exp_a[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)];\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] eb = rg_exp_b[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)];\n            ks_add #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_eadd (\n                .a({ea[EXP_WIDTH_I], ea}),\n                .b({eb[EXP_WIDTH_I], eb}),\n                .cin(1'b0),\n                .s(rg_exp_c[gj*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_exp;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_ma, pipe1_mb;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_macc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_eacc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs <= signs;\n        pipe1_exp   <= rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_ma    <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mb    <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_macc  <= mant_acc;\n        pipe1_eacc  <= rg_exp_acc;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R2: multiply || max tree || popcount   (pipe1 -> pipe2)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] prod;\n    generate\n        genvar gk;\n        for (gk = 0; gk < N; gk++) begin : g_mul\n            assign prod[gk*MUL_W +: MUL_W] =\n                pipe1_ma[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe1_mb[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    logic [NL*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] items;\n    generate\n        genvar gl;\n        for (gl = 0; gl < N; gl++) begin : g_it\n            assign items[gl*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_exp[gl*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign items[N*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] = pipe1_eacc;\n\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] emax;\n    maxtree #(.N(NL), .W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_maxtree (.items(items), .mx(emax));\n\n    wire [CARRY_W-1:0] kpop;\n    popcount #(.N(NL)) u_pop (.v(pipe1_signs), .cnt(kpop));\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] pipe2_prod;\n    logic [NL*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe2_items;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_max;\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic [CARRY_W-1:0] pipe2_k;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_macc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_prod  <= prod;\n        pipe2_items <= items;\n        pipe2_max   <= emax;\n        pipe2_signs <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_k     <= kpop;\n        pipe2_macc  <= pipe1_macc;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R3: subtract + align + fold + compress   (pipe2 -> pipe3)\n    // ********************\n    // pre-alignment products, left justified in ALIGN_WIDTH bits\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] prod14;\n    generate\n        genvar gm;\n        for (gm = 0; gm < N; gm++) begin : g_p14\n            assign prod14[gm*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n                {pipe2_prod[gm*MUL_W +: MUL_W], {(ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_W){1'b0}}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    generate\n        genvar go;\n        for (go = 0; go < NL; go++) begin : g_sh\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] dv;\n            ks_add #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_sub (\n                .a(pipe2_max),\n                .b(~pipe2_items[go*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .cin(1'b1),\n                .s(dv)\n            );\n            // dv >= 14 already shifts all row bits out; dv[3:0] is used as the shift amount\n            wire big = dv[6] | dv[5] | dv[4];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pv = prod14[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh = pv >> dv[ASH_W-1:0];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] shz = sh & ~{ALIGN_WIDTH{big}};\n            assign folded[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = shz ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[go]}};\n        end\n [(NL+1)*ROW_W-1:0] csa_rows;\n    generate\n        genvar gp;\n        for (gp = 0; gp < NL; gp++) begin : g_rows\n            assign csa_rows[gp*ROW_W +: ROW_W] = {{(ROW_W-ALIGN_WIDTH){1'b0}}, (.rows(csa_rows), .s_o(csa_s), .c_o(csa_c));\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [ROW_W-1:0] pipe3_s, pipe3_c;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_max;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_s ********************\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] sum_full;\n    ks_add #(.W(ROW_W)) u_cpa (.a(pipe3_s), .b(pipe3_c), .cin(1'b0), .s(sum_full));\n\n    wire p4sign = sum_full[ROW_W-1];\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] Balanced pipeline + parallel-prefix arithmetic:\n//   R1 in->pipe1 : decode (33x) + product-exponent add\n//   R2 pipe1->2  : 16x (4x4 mul) || max tree (16x7, prefix compare) || popcount\n//   R3 parallel-prefix adder: s = a + b + cin (mod 2^W)\nmodule ks_add #(\n    parameter int unsigned W = 8\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [W-1:0] b,\n    input  logic         cin,\n    output logic [W-1:0] s\n);\n    localparam int unsigned L = pdpu_pkg::clog2(W);\n    logic [(L+1)*W-1:0] G, P;\n    genvar gi, gl;\n    generate\n        for (gi = 0; gi < W; gi++) begin : g_init\n            if (gi == 0) begin : g_cin\n                assign G[0] = (a[0] & b[0]) | ((a[0] ^ b[0]) & cin);\n            end else begin : g_std\n                assign G[gi] = a[gi] & b[gi];\n            end\n            assign P[gi] = a[gi] ^ b[gi];\n        end\n        if (W == 1) begin : g_w1\n            assign s[0] = a[0] ^ b[0] ^ cin;\n        end else begin : g_main\n            for (gl = 1; gl <= L; gl++) begin : g_lvl\n                localparam int unsigned SP = 1 << (gl-1);\n                for (gi = 0; gi < W; gi++) begin : g_bit\n                    if (gi < SP) begin : g_pass\n                        assign G[gl*W + gi] = G[(gl-1)*W + gi];\n                        assign P[gl*W + gi] = P[(gl-1)*W + gi];\n                    end else begin : g_comb\n                        assign G[gl*W + gi] = G[(gl-1)*W + gi] | (P[(gl-1)*W + gi] & G[(gl-1)*W + gi - SP]);\n                        assign P[gl*W + gi] = P[(gl-1)*W + gi] & P[(gl-1)*W + gi - SP];\n                    end\n                end\n            end\n            // s[i] = P0[i] ^ carry-into-bit-i; carry into bit 0 is cin\n            assign s = P[W-1:0] ^ {G[L*W + W-2 -: W-1], cin};\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// 2-input signed max via prefix (Kogge-Stone) magnitude comparator\nmodule max2p #(\n    parameter int unsigned W = 7\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [W-1:0] b,\n    output logic [W-1:0] mx\n);\n    localparam int unsigned M = W-1;\n    localparam int unsigned L = pdpu_pkg::clog2(M);\n    logic [(L+1)*M-1:0] G, P;\n    genvar gi, gl;\n    generate\n        for (gi = 0; gi < M; gi++) begin : g_init\n            assign G[gi] = b[gi] & ~a[gi];\n            assign P[gi] = ~(a[gi] ^ b[gi]);\n        end\n        for (gl = 1; gl <= L; gl++) begin : g_lvl\n            localparam int unsigned SP = 1 << (gl-1);\n            for (gi = 0; gi < M; gi++) begin : g_bit\n                if (gi < SP) begin : g_pass\n                    assign G[gl*M + gi] = G[(gl-1)*M + gi];\n                    assign P[gl*M + gi] = P[(gl-1)*M + gi];\n                end else begin : g_comb\n                    assign G[gl*M + gi] = G[(gl-1)*M + gi] | (P[(gl-1)*M + gi] & G[(gl-1)*M + gi - SP]);\n                    assign P[gl*M + gi] = P[(gl-1)*M + gi] & P[(gl-1)*M + gi - SP];\n                end\n            end\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    wire gt = G[L*M + M-1];              // b[M-2:0] > a[M-2:0] unsigned\n    wire sdiff = a[W-1] ^ b[W-1];\n    wire sel = (b[W-1] & ~a[W-1]) | (~sdiff & gt);\n    assign mx = sel ? b : a;\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n)(\n    input logic [n-1:0] operand_i,\n    output logic sign_o,\n    output logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_o,\n    output logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_o\n);\n    logic sign;\n    logic [n-2:0] z, operand_value;\n    assign sign = operand_i[n-1];\n    assign z = operand_i[n-2:0];\n    // (-z)[i] = z[i] ^ |z[i-1:0]| (i>=1), (-z)[0] = z[0]\n    logic [n-2:0] S;\n    or_scan #(.M(n-1)) u_scan (.x(z), .y(S));\n    assign operand_value = z ^ ({(n-1){sign}} & {S[n-3:0], 1'b0});\n\n    logic regS;\n    logic [n-2:0] lzc_operand;\n    logic [nd-1:0] leading_zero_count;\n    logic lzc_zeroes;\n    assign regS = operand_value[n-2];\n    assign lzc_operand = regS ? (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    logic signed [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_k;\n    logic signed [es:0] exp;\n    assign regime_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    end\n\n    logic sign_k;\n    logic [n-2:0] rg_const;\n    logic [n-2:0] regime;\n    assign sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    assign rg_const = 1;\n    assign regime = sign_k ? rg_const : ~rg_const;\n\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] regime_bits;\n    assign regime_bits = sign_k ? (~regime_k+2) : (regime_k+2);\n\n    localparam int unsigned TEMP_WIDTH = (n-1)+es+MANT_WIDTH;\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH-1:0] rg_exp_mant;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign rg_exp_mant = {regime, exp[es-1:0], mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n    end\n\n    localparam int unsigned MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT = MANT_WIDTH + es + 1;\n    localparam int unsigned SHIFT_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::clog2(MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+1);\n    logic [SHIFT_WIDTH-1:0] shift_amount;\n    assign shift_amount = (regime_bits>=n) ? MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT : (regime_bits+(MANT_WIDTH+es-n+1));\n\n    logic [TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] value_before_shift, value_after_shift;\n    assign value_before_shift = rg_exp_mant << MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(TEMP_WIDTH+MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(SHIFT_WIDTH),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(value_before_shift),\n        .shift_amount(shift_amount),\n        .result_o(value_after_shift)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-2:0] value_before_round;\n    logic [MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1:0] rounding_bits;\n    assign {value_before_round,rounding_bits} = value_after_shift[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT+n-2:0];\n\n    logic round_bit;\n    logic sticky_bit;\n    logic round_value;\n    assign round_bit = rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-1];\n    assign sticky_bit = |rounding_bits[MAX_SHIFT_AMOUNT-2:0];\n    assign round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | value_before_round[0]);\n\n    // merged: normal_result[n-2:0] = sign_i ? -(vbr+rv) : (vbr+rv)\n    //       = (vbr ^ {sign}) + (rv ^ sign)          (exact in Z/(2^(n-1)))\n    logic [n-2:0] add_a, add_s;\n    assign add_a = value_before_round ^ {(n-1){sign_i}};\n    ks_add #(\n        .W(n-1)\n    ) u_round_add (\n        .a(add_a),\n        .b({{(n-2){1'b0}}, round_value ^ sign_i}),\n        .cin(1'b0),\n        .s(add_s)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = {sign_i, add_s};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\n// balanced popcount tree\nmodule popcount #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17\n)(\n    input  logic [N-1:0] v,\n    output logic [pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1)-1:0] cnt\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign cnt = v[0];\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            assign cnt = v[0] + v[1];\n        end else begin : split\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NA+1)-1:0] ca;\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NB+1)-1:0] cb;\n            popcount #(.N(NA)) ua (.v(v[0 +: NA]), .cnt(ca));\n            popcount #(.N(NB)) ub (.v(v[NA +: NB]), .cnt(cb));\n            assign cnt = ca + cb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// greedy Wallace reduction of N rows (width W) down to two rows using 3:2\n// compressors (full adders, no serial carry chains). Depth = 6 for N=18.\nmodule csa_reduce #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 18,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 19\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] rows,\n    output logic [W-1:0] s_o,\n    output logic [W-1:0] c_o\n);\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : b1\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = '0;\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : b2\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = rows[W +: W];\n        end else begin : rec\n            localparam int unsigned M = N/3;\n            localparam int unsigned REM = N - 3*M;\n            localparam int unsigned NN = 2*M + REM;\n            wire [M*W-1:0] ts, tc;\n            genvar j;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : trip\n                wire [W-1:0] ra = rows[(3*j+0)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rb = rows[(3*j+1)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rc = rows[(3*j+2)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] ab = ra & rb;\n                wire [W-1:0] aob = ra | rb;\n                assign ts[j*W +: W] = ra ^ rb ^ rc;\n                assign tc[j*W +: W] = {((ab[W-2:0]) | (rc[W-2:0] & aob[W-2:0])), 1'b0};\n            end\n            wire [NN*W-1:0] nxt;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : pack\n                assign nxt[(2*j)*W +: W] = ts[j*W +: W];\n                assign nxt[(2*j+1)*W +: W] = tc[j*W +: W];\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < REM; j++) begin : pass\n                assign nxt[(2*M+j)*W +: W] = rows[(3*M+j)*W +: W];\n            end\n            csa_reduce #(.N(NN), .W(W)) sub (.rows(nxt), .s_o(s_o), .c_o(c_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i - es_i - 3;                  // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o - es_o - 3;                  // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1, EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MUL_W = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned CARRY_W = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);                 // 5\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_W = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;                  // 19\n    localparam int unsigned NL = N + 1;                                     // 17\n    localparam int unsigned ROW_W = SUM_W;                                  // 19\n\n    // ********************\n    // R1: decode + product exponent add   (in -> pipe1)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    wire sign_acc;\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_acc;\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o)) u_dec_acc (\n        .operand_i(acc), .sign_o(sign_acc), .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc), .mant_norm_o(mant_acc)\n    );\n\n    generate\n        genvar gi;\n        for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi++) begin : g_dec\n            wire sg_a, sg_b;\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] re_a, re_b;\n            wire [MANT_WIDTH_I:0] mn_a, mn_b;\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_a),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_a), .mant_norm_o(mn_a)\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_b),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_b), .mant_norm_o(mn_b)\n            );\n            assign signs_a[gi] = sg_a;\n            assign signs_b[gi] = sg_b;\n            assign rg_exp_a[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_a;\n            assign rg_exp_b[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_b;\n            assign mants_a[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_a;\n            assign mants_b[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_b;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    wire [N-1:0] signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    wire [N:0] signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar gj;\n        for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj++) begin : g_eadd\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] ea = rg_exp_a[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)];\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] eb = rg_exp_b[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)];\n            ks_add #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_eadd (\n                .a({ea[EXP_WIDTH_I], ea}),\n                .b({eb[EXP_WIDTH_I], eb}),\n                .cin(1'b0),\n                .s(rg_exp_c[gj*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_exp;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_ma, pipe1_mb;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_macc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_eacc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs <= signs;\n        pipe1_exp   <= rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_ma    <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mb    <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_macc  <= mant_acc;\n        pipe1_eacc  <= rg_exp_acc;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R2: multiply || max tree (16 items) || popcount   (pipe1 -> pipe2)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] prod;\n    generate\n        genvar gk;\n        for (gk = 0; gk < N; gk++) begin : g_mul\n            assign prod[gk*MUL_W +: MUL_W] =\n                pipe1_ma[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe1_mb[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // max tree over the 16 product exponents: 4 levels of max2p\n    wire [N-1:0][EXP_WIDTH:0] t1, t2, t3;\n    generate\n        genvar gm2;\n        for (gm2 = 0; gm2 < N/2; gm2++) begin : g_l1\n            max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max (\n                .a(pipe1_exp[(2*gm2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .b(pipe1_exp[(2*gm2+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .mx(t1[gm2])\n            );\n        end\n        for (gm2 = 0; gm2 < N/4; gm2++) begin : g_l2\n            max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max (.a(t1[2*gm2]), .b(t1[2*gm2+1]), .mx(t2[gm2]));\n        end\n        for (gm2 = 0; gm2 < N/8; gm2++) begin : g_l3\n            max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max (.a(t2[2*gm2]), .b(t2[2*gm2+1]), .mx(t3[gm2]));\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] emax16;\n    max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max_l4 (.a(t3[0]), .b(t3[1]), .mx(emax16));\n\n    wire [CARRY_W-1:0] kpop;\n    popcount #(.N(NL)) u_pop (.v(pipe1_signs), .cnt(kpop));\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] pipe2_prod;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe2_exp;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_eacc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_emax16;\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic [CARRY_W-1:0] pipe2_k;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_macc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_prod   <= prod;\n        pipe2_exp    <= pipe1_exp;\n        pipe2_eacc   <= pipe1_eacc;\n        pipe2_emax16 <= emax16;\n        pipe2_signs  <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_k      <= kpop;\n        pipe2_macc   <= pipe1_macc;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R3: acc max + subtract + clamp + align shift (hi)   (pipe2 -> pipe3)\n    // ********************\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] emax;\n    max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max_acc (.a(pipe2_emax16), .b(pipe2_eacc), .mx(emax));\n\n    // pre-alignment rows, left justified in ALIGN_WIDTH bits\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] prod14;\n    generate\n        genvar gn;\n        for (gn = 0; gn < N; gn++) begin : g_p14\n            assign prod14[gn*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n                {pipe2_prod[gn*MUL_W +: MUL_W], {(ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_W){1'b0}}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign prod14[N*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = {1'b0, pipe2_macc, 1'b0};\n\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] partial;\n    logic [NL-1:0][1:0] dv_lo;\n    generate\n        genvar go;\n        for (go = 0; go < NL; go++) begin : g_sub_sh\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] dv;\n            ks_add #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_sub (\n                .a(emax),\n                .b(~pipe2_exp[go*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .cin(1'b1),\n                .s(dv)\n            );\n            wire [3:0] dvc = (|dv[EXP_WIDTH:4]) ? 4'd15 : dv[3:0];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pv = prod14[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh0 = pv;\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh4 = {4'b0, pv[ALIGN_WIDTH-1:4]};\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh8 = {8'b0, pv[ALIGN_WIDTH-1:8]};\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh12 = {12'b0, pv[ALIGN_WIDTH-1:12]};\n            assign partial[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n                (dvc[3:2] == 2'd0) ? sh0 : (dvc[3:2] == 2'd1) ? sh4 :\n                (dvc[3:2] == 2'd2) ? sh8 : sh12;\n            assign dv_lo[go] = dvc[1:0];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pipe3_partial;\n    logic [NL-1:0][1:0] pipe3_dlo;\n    logic [N:0] pipe3_signs;\n    logic [CARRY_W-1:0] pipe3_k;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe3_macc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_max;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_partial <= partial;\n        pipe3_dlo     <= dv_lo;\n        pipe3_signs   <= pipe2_signs;\n        pipe3_k       <= pipe2_k;\n        pipe3_macc    <= pipe2_macc;\n        pipe3_max     <= emax;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R4: shift (lo) + fold + compress + CPA   (pipe3 -> pipe4)\n    // ********************\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] folded;\n    generate\n        genvar gp;\n        for (gp = 0; gp < NL; gp++) begin : g_sh2\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pv = pipe3_partial[gp*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s0 = pv;\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s1 = {1'b0, pv[ALIGN_WIDTH-1:1]};\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s2 = {2'b0, pv[ALIGN_WIDTH-1:2]};\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] s3 = {3'b0, pv[ALIGN_WIDTH-1:3]};\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh = (pipe3_dlo[gp] == 2'd0) ? s0 :\n                                        (pipe3_dlo[gp] == 2'd1) ? s1 :\n                                        (pipe3_dlo[gp] == 2'd2) ? s2 : s3;\n            assign [ASSISTANT]
Found the real bug: in `max2p` the sign-mismatch case is **inverted** — I select `b` when `b` is negative (`b_sign & ~a_sign`), but the reference comparator selects the *positive* operand as the max. Correct: `sel = a_sign & ~b_sign | (~sdiff & gt)`:

[ASSISTANT]
design5 (780k) is best. Now I'll build a fully rebalanced version (design7) with: the exponent-diff moved to R2, the absolute value computed as **two parallel KS adds** in R4 (S and −S, avoiding a serial negate), a merged `fre` add, and a **cheap encoder** (I derived that the encoder's 46-bit shift reduces to one 31-bit left-barrel by `16−sa`, with `sa = min((k^sign)+2, 16)` — eliminating the regime-bit and shift-amount adders):
  -> create_file(content="// pdpu_top_pipelined -- 5-stage pipelined 16-lane posit dot-product-accumulate.\n// Bit-exact to the combinational reference pdpu_dot16 (ALIGN_WIDTH=14 truncation,\n// NaR handling identical). Balanced pipeline + parallel-prefix arithmetic:\n//   R1 in->pipe1 : 33x posit decode + 16x product-exponent add (Kogge-Stone)\n//   R2 pipe1->2  : 16x (4x4 mul) || max tree (prefix compare) || 17x exp diff\n//   R3 pipe2->3  : align shift + sign fold + corr row + CSA compress (18 rows)\n//   R4 pipe3->4  : carry-propagate add + negated sum (parallel) + abs mux\n//   R5 pipe4->5  : LZC + merged final-exponent add || normalize shift\n//   R6 pipe5->out: posit encode (single 31-bit barrel, no regime/shift adders)\n// Per-lane two's-complement adders are replaced by XOR-folding plus a single\n// popcount correction row folded into the compressor tree (exact in Z/(2^19)).\n\npackage pdpu_pkg;\n    function automatic int maximum(int a, int b);\n        maximum = (a > b) ? a : b;\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog2(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog2=0; n>0; clog2=clog2+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\npackage cf_math_pkg;\n    function automatic integer ceil_div (input longint dividend, input longint divisor);\n        longint remainder;\n        remainder = dividend;\n        for (ceil_div = 0; remainder > 0; ceil_div++) begin\n            remainder = remainder - divisor;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer clog(input integer n);\n        begin\n            n = n-1;\n            for(clog=0; n>0; clog=clog+1)\n                n = n>>1;\n        end\n    endfunction\n    function automatic integer unsigned idx_width (input integer unsigned num_idx);\n        idx_width = (num_idx > 32'd1) ? $unsigned(clog(num_idx)) : 32'd1;\n    endfunction\nendpackage\n\nmodule lzc #(\n  parameter int unsigned WIDTH = 2,\n  parameter bit empty_o\n);\n  if (WIDTH == 1) begin : gen_degenerate_lzc\n    assign cnt_o[0] = !in_i[0];\n    assign empty_o = !in_i[0];\n  end else begin : gen_lzc\n    localparam int unsigned NumLevels = < WIDTH; j++) begin : g_index_lut\n      assign index_lut[(j)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = ($unsigned(j));\n    end\n    for (genvar level = 0; $unsigned(level) < NumLevels; level++) begin : g_levels\n      if ($unsigned(level) == NumLevels - 1) begin : g_last_level\n        for (genvar k = 0; k < 2 ** level; k++) begin : g_level\n          if ($unsigned(k) * 2 < WIDTH - 1) begin : g_reduce\n            assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2] | in_tmp[k * 2 + 1];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = (in_tmp[k * 2] == 1'b1)\n              ? assign sel_nodes[2 ** level - 1 + k] = in_tmp[k * 2];\n            assign index_nodes[(2 ** level - 1 + k)*NumLevels +: NumLevels] = index_lut[(k * 2)*NumLevels +: NumLevels];\n          end\n assign t[gl*M + gi] = t[(gl-1)*M + gi] | t[(gl-1)*M + gi - SP];\n                end\n            end\n        end\n        assign y = t[L*M +: M];\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// Kogge-Stone parallel-prefix adder: s = a + b + cin (mod 2^W)\nmodule ks_add #(\n    parameter int unsigned W = 8\n)(\n    input  logic [W-1:0] a,\n    input  logic [W-1:0] b,\n    input  logic         cin,\n end\n            assign P[gi] = a[gi] ^ b[gi];\n        end\n        if (W == 1) begin : g_w1\n            assign s[0] = a[0] ^ b[0] ^ cin;\n        end else begin : g_main\n            for (gl = 1; G[gl*W + gi] = G[(gl-1)*W + gi] | (P[(gl-1)*W + gi] & G[(gl-1)*W + gi - SP]);\n                        assign P[gl*W + gi] = P[(gl-1)*W + gi] & P[(gl-1)*W + gi - SP];\n                    end\n gt);\n    assign mx = sel ? b : a;\nendmodule\n\nmodule posit_decoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = (~operand_value) : operand_value;\n    lzc #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .MODE(1'b1)\n    ) u_lzc(\n        .in_i(lzc_operand),\n        .cnt_o(leading_zero_count),\n        .empty_o(lzc_zeroes)\n    );\n\n    logic [nd-1:0] runlength;\n    logic [nd-1:0] regime_bits;\n    logic signed [nd:0] regime_k;\n    assign runlength = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : leading_zero_count;\n    assign regime_bits = lzc_zeroes ? (n-1) : (leading_zero_count+1);\n    assign regime_k = regS ? ({1'b0,runlength-1}) : ({1'b1,~runlength+1});\n\n    logic [n-2:0] op_no_rg;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(n-1),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(nd),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_barrel_shifter(\n        .operand_i(operand_value),\n        .shift_amount(regime_bits),\n        .result_o(op_no_rg)\n    );\n\n    assign sign_o = sign;\n\n    logic [es:0] exp;\n    if(es==0) begin\n        assign exp = 0;\n    end\n    else begin\n        assign exp = op_no_rg[n-2:n-2-es+1];\n    end\n    assign rg_exp_o = regime_k << es | exp;\n\n    logic implicit_bit;\n    assign implicit_bit = |operand_i[n-2:0];\n    assign mant_norm_o = {implicit_bit, op_no_rg[n-2-es:2]};\nendmodule\n\n// balanced popcount tree\nmodule popcount #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 17\n)(\n    input  logic [N-1:0] v,\n    output logic [pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1)-1:0] cnt\n);\n    localparam int unsigned NA = N/2;\n    localparam int unsigned NB = N - NA;\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : leaf\n            assign cnt = v[0];\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : pair\n            assign cnt = v[0] + v[1];\n        end else begin : split\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NA+1)-1:0] ca;\n            wire [pdpu_pkg::clog2(NB+1)-1:0] cb;\n            popcount #(.N(NA)) ua (.v(v[0 +: NA]), .cnt(ca));\n            popcount #(.N(NB)) ub (.v(v[NA +: NB]), .cnt(cb));\n            assign cnt = ca + cb;\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// greedy Wallace reduction of N rows (width W) down to two rows using 3:2\n// compressors (full adders, no serial carry chains). Depth = 6 for N=18.\nmodule csa_reduce #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 18,\n    parameter int unsigned W = 19\n)(\n    input  logic [N*W-1:0] rows,\n    output logic [W-1:0] s_o,\n    output logic [W-1:0] c_o\n);\n    generate\n        if (N == 1) begin : b1\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = '0;\n        end else if (N == 2) begin : b2\n            assign s_o = rows[0 +: W];\n            assign c_o = rows[W +: W];\n        end else begin : rec\n            localparam int unsigned M = N/3;\n            localparam int unsigned REM = N - 3*M;\n            localparam int unsigned NN = 2*M + REM;\n            wire [M*W-1:0] ts, tc;\n            genvar j;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : trip\n                wire [W-1:0] ra = rows[(3*j+0)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rb = rows[(3*j+1)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] rc = rows[(3*j+2)*W +: W];\n                wire [W-1:0] ab = ra & rb;\n                wire [W-1:0] aob = ra | rb;\n                assign ts[j*W +: W] = ra ^ rb ^ rc;\n                assign tc[j*W +: W] = {((ab[W-2:0]) | (rc[W-2:0] & aob[W-2:0])), 1'b0};\n            end\n            wire [NN*W-1:0] nxt;\n            for (j = 0; j < M; j++) begin : pack\n                assign nxt[(2*j)*W +: W] = ts[j*W +: W];\n                assign nxt[(2*j+1)*W +: W] = tc[j*W +: W];\n            end\n            for (j = 0; j < REM; j++) begin : pass\n                assign nxt[(2*M+j)*W +: W] = rows[(3*M+j)*W +: W];\n            end\n            csa_reduce #(.N(NN), .W(W)) sub (.rows(nxt), .s_o(s_o), .c_o(c_o));\n        end\n    endgenerate\nendmodule\n\n// positure encoder, restructured: the reference computes\n//   X = {14{~sign_k}, sign_k, exp[1:0], mant[12:0]}  (30b, regime pattern fill)\n//   U = X << (16 - sa) with sa = min(regime_bits,16), regime_bits = (k^sign)+2\n//   out = U[30:16], round_bit = U[15], sticky = |U[14:0]\nmodule posit_encoder #(\n    parameter int unsigned n = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es = 1,\n    parameter int unsigned nd = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n-1),\n    parameter int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = nd+es,\n    parameter int unsigned MANT_WIDTH = n-es-3\n) (\n    input logic sign_i,\n    input logic signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] rg_exp_i,\n    input logic [MANT_WIDTH:0] mant_norm_i,\n    output logic [n-1:0] result_o\n);\n    logic input_not_zero;\n    assign input_not_zero = mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH];\n\n    // sa = min(regime_bits, 16); regime_bits = (k ^ {5{k[4]}}) + 2, k = rg_exp_i[6:2]\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] kk = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH:es];       // regime_k\n    wire ksign = kk[EXP_WIDTH-es];\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] ka = kk ^ {(EXP_WIDTH-es+1){ksign}};\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] rb = ka + {{(EXP_WIDTH-es-1){1'b0}}, 2'b10};\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH-es:0] sa = rb[EXP_WIDTH-es] ? {{(EXP_WIDTH-es-1){1'b0}}, 4'b0000, 1'b1} : rb;\n\n    // X = {14{~sign_k}, sign_k, exp, mant[12:0]}\n    localparam int unsigned XW = (n-1) + es + MANT_WIDTH;    // 30\n    wire sign_k = rg_exp_i[EXP_WIDTH];\n    wire [es-1:0] expf = rg_exp_i[es-1:0];\n    wire [XW-1:0] xv = {{(n-2){~sign_k}}, sign_k, expf, mant_norm_i[MANT_WIDTH-1:0]};\n\n    // U = X << (16 - sa), truncated to 31 bits\n    localparam int unsigned UW = MAXUW;\n    wire [UW-1:0] uv;\n    generate\n        if (UW == 31) begin : g_u31\n            wire [30:0] pre;\n            assign pre[30:16] = '0;\n            assign pre[15:0] = xv[15:0];\n            // left shift by (16 - sa) in [0,15]\n            wire [30:0] s1 = sa[0] ? {pre[29:0], 1'b0} : pre;\n            wire [30:0] s2 = sa[1] ? {s1[27:0], 4'b0} : s1;\n            wire [30:0] s3 = sa[2] ? {s1[19:0], 12'b0} : s2;\n            wire [30:0] s4 = sa[3] ? {s1[30-15 -: 16], 16'b0} : s3;\n            assign uv = s4;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    wire [n-2:0] vbr = uv[UW-1 -: (n-1)];\n    wire round_bit = uv[15];\n    wire sticky_bit = |uv[14:0];\n    wire round_value = round_bit & (sticky_bit | vbr[0]);\n\n    // merged: normal_result[n-2:0] = sign_i ? -(vbr+rv) : (vbr+rv)\n    wire [n-2:0] add_a, add_s;\n    assign add_a = vbr ^ {(n-1){sign_i}};\n    ks_add #(\n        .W(n-1)\n    ) u_round_add (\n        .a(add_a),\n        .b({{(n-2){1'b0}}, round_value ^ sign_i}),\n        .cin(1'b0),\n        .s(add_s)\n    );\n\n    logic [n-1:0] normal_result;\n    assign normal_result = {sign_i, add_s};\n    assign result_o = input_not_zero ? normal_result : '0;\nendmodule\n\nmodule pdpu_top_pipelined #(\n    parameter int unsigned N = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned n_i = 8,\n    parameter int unsigned es_i = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned n_o = 16,\n    parameter int unsigned es_o = 2,\n    parameter int unsigned ALIGN_WIDTH = 14\n)(\n    input  logic clk,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_a,\n    input  logic [N*n_i-1:0] operands_b,\n    input  logic [n_o-1:0]   acc,\n    output logic [n_o-1:0]   result_o\n);\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_I = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_i-1) + es_i;   // 5\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_I = n_i - es_i - 3;                  // 3\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH_O = pdpu_pkg::clog2(n_o-1) + es_o;   // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MANT_WIDTH_O = n_o - es_o - 3;                  // 11\n    localparam int unsigned EXP_WIDTH = pdpu_pkg::maximum(EXP_WIDTH_I+1, EXP_WIDTH_O); // 6\n    localparam int unsigned MUL_W = 2*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1);                     // 8\n    localparam int unsigned CARRY_W = pdpu_pkg::clog2(N+1);                 // 5\n    localparam int unsigned SUM_W = ALIGN_WIDTH + CARRY_W;                  // 19\n    localparam int unsigned NL = N + 1;                                     // 17\n    localparam int unsigned ROW_W = SUM_W;                                  // 19\n\n    // ********************\n    // R1: decode + product exponent add   (in -> pipe1)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N-1:0] signs_a, signs_b;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] rg_exp_a, rg_exp_b;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] mants_a, mants_b;\n\n    wire sign_acc;\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] rg_exp_acc;\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] mant_acc;\n    posit_decoder #(.n(n_o), .es(es_o)) u_dec_acc (\n        .operand_i(acc), .sign_o(sign_acc), .rg_exp_o(rg_exp_acc), .mant_norm_o(mant_acc)\n    );\n\n    generate\n        genvar gi;\n        for (gi = 0; gi < N; gi++) begin : g_dec\n            wire sg_a, sg_b;\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] re_a, re_b;\n            wire [MANT_WIDTH_I:0] mn_a, mn_b;\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ua_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_a[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_a),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_a), .mant_norm_o(mn_a)\n            );\n            posit_decoder #(.n(n_i), .es(es_i)) ub_posit_decoder (\n                .operand_i(operands_b[gi*n_i +: n_i]), .sign_o(sg_b),\n                .rg_exp_o(re_b), .mant_norm_o(mn_b)\n            );\n            assign signs_a[gi] = sg_a;\n            assign signs_b[gi] = sg_b;\n            assign rg_exp_a[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_a;\n            assign rg_exp_b[gi*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)] = re_b;\n            assign mants_a[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_a;\n            assign mants_b[gi*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] = mn_b;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    wire [N-1:0] signs_ab = signs_a ^ signs_b;\n    wire [N:0] signs = {sign_acc, signs_ab};\n\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] rg_exp_c;\n    generate\n        genvar gj;\n        for (gj = 0; gj < N; gj++) begin : g_eadd\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] ea = rg_exp_a[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)];\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH_I:0] eb = rg_exp_b[gj*(EXP_WIDTH_I+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH_I+1)];\n            ks_add #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_eadd (\n                .a({ea[EXP_WIDTH_I], ea}),\n                .b({eb[EXP_WIDTH_I], eb}),\n                .cin(1'b0),\n                .s(rg_exp_c[gj*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)])\n            );\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe1 ----\n    logic [N:0] pipe1_signs;\n    logic [N*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] pipe1_exp;\n    logic [N*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1)-1:0] pipe1_ma, pipe1_mb;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_macc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH_O:0] pipe1_eacc;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe1_signs <= signs;\n        pipe1_exp   <= rg_exp_c;\n        pipe1_ma    <= mants_a;\n        pipe1_mb    <= mants_b;\n        pipe1_macc  <= mant_acc;\n        pipe1_eacc  <= rg_exp_acc;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R2: multiply || max tree || popcount || exponent diff  (pipe1 -> pipe2)\n    // ********************\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] prod;\n    generate\n        genvar gk;\n        for (gk = 0; gk < N; gk++) begin : g_mul\n            assign prod[gk*MUL_W +: MUL_W] =\n                pipe1_ma[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)] *\n                pipe1_mb[gk*(MANT_WIDTH_I+1) +: (MANT_WIDTH_I+1)];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // max tree over the 16 product exponents (4 levels of max2p)\n    wire [N-1:0][EXP_WIDTH:0] t1, t2, t3;\n    generate\n        genvar gm2;\n        for (gm2 = 0; gm2 < N/2; gm2++) begin : g_l1\n            max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max (\n                .a(pipe1_exp[(2*gm2)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .b(pipe1_exp[(2*gm2+1)*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .mx(t1[gm2])\n            );\n        end\n        for (gm2 = 0; gm2 < N/4; gm2++) begin : g_l2\n            max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max (.a(t1[2*gm2]), .b(t1[2*gm2+1]), .mx(t2[gm2]));\n        end\n        for (gm2 = 0; gm2 < N/8; gm2++) begin : g_l3\n            max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max (.a(t2[2*gm2]), .b(t2[2*gm2+1]), .mx(t3[gm2]));\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] emax16;\n    max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max_l4 (.a(t3[0]), .b(t3[1]), .mx(emax16));\n\n    wire [CARRY_W-1:0] kpop;\n    popcount #(.N(NL)) u_pop (.v(pipe1_signs), .cnt(kpop));\n\n    // 17-entry exponent vector (products + accumulator)\n    wire [NL*(EXP_WIDTH+1)-1:0] items2;\n    generate\n        genvar gie;\n        for (gie = 0; gie < NL; gie++) begin : g_it2\n            assign items2[gie*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] =\n                (gie < N) ? pipe1_exp[gie*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)] : pipe1_eacc;\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // exponent differences + clamp: dvc = min(dv,15), dv = emax - item\n    wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] emax;\n    max2p #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_max_acc (.a(emax16), .b(pipe1_eacc), .mx(emax));\n\n    logic [NL*4-1:0] dvc;\n    generate\n        genvar gn;\n        for (gn = 0; gn < NL; gn++) begin : g_sub\n            wire [EXP_WIDTH:0] dv;\n            ks_add #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_sub (\n                .a(emax),\n                .b(~items2[gn*(EXP_WIDTH+1) +: (EXP_WIDTH+1)]),\n                .cin(1'b1),\n                .s(dv)\n            );\n            assign dvc[gn*4 +: 4] = (|dv[EXP_WIDTH:4]) ? 4'hF : dv[3:0];\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // ---- pipe2 ----\n    logic [N*MUL_W-1:0] pipe2_prod;\n    logic [NL*4-1:0] pipe2_dvc;\n    logic [N:0] pipe2_signs;\n    logic [CARRY_W-1:0] pipe2_k;\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O:0] pipe2_macc;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe2_max;\n\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe2_prod  <= prod;\n        pipe2_dvc   <= dvc;\n        pipe2_signs <= pipe1_signs;\n        pipe2_k     <= kpop;\n        pipe2_macc  <= pipe1_macc;\n        pipe2_max   <= emax;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R3: align + fold + compress   (pipe2 -> pipe3)\n    // ********************\n    // pre-alignment products, left justified in ALIGN_WIDTH bits\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] prod14;\n    generate\n        genvar gm;\n        for (gm = 0; gm < N; gm++) begin : g_p14\n            assign prod14[gm*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] =\n                {pipe2_prod[gm*MUL_W +: MUL_W], {(ALIGN_WIDTH-MUL_W){1'b0}}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign prod14[N*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = {1'b0, pipe2_macc, 1'b0};\n\n    // align shift + sign fold\n    logic [NL*ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] folded;\n    generate\n        genvar go;\n        for (go = 0; go < NL; go++) begin : g_sh\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] pv = prod14[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH];\n            wire [3:0] sa3 = pipe2_dvc[go*4 +: 4];\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh0 = pv;\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh8 = {8'b0, pv[ALIGN_WIDTH-1:8]};\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh4 = sa3[2] ? sh8 : pv;\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh2 = {2'b0, sh4[ALIGN_WIDTH-1:2]};\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh3 = sa3[1] ? sh2 : sh4;\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] sh1 = {1'b0, sh3[ALIGN_WIDTH-1:1]};\n            wire [ALIGN_WIDTH-1:0] shf = sa3[0] ? sh1 : sh3;\n            assign folded[go*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH] = shf ^ {ALIGN_WIDTH{pipe2_signs[go]}};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n\n    // correction row: -k*2^ALIGN_WIDTH + k (mod 2^ROW_W)\n    wire [5:0] nc6 = -{1'b0, pipe2_k};\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] corr = {nc6, {(ROW_W-6-CARRY_W){1'b0}}, pipe2_k};\n\n    logic [(NL+1)*ROW_W-1:0] csa_rows;\n    generate\n        genvar gp;\n        for (gp = 0; gp < NL; gp++) begin : g_rows\n            assign csa_rows[gp*ROW_W +: ROW_W] = {{(ROW_W-ALIGN_WIDTH){1'b0}}, folded[gp*ALIGN_WIDTH +: ALIGN_WIDTH]};\n        end\n    endgenerate\n    assign csa_rows[NL*ROW_W +: ROW_W] = corr;\n\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] csa_s, csa_c;\n    csa_reduce #(.N(NL+1), .W(ROW_W)) u_csa (.rows(csa_rows), .s_o(csa_s), .c_o(csa_c));\n\n    // ---- pipe3 ----\n    logic [ROW_W-1:0] pipe3_s, pipe3_c;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe3_max;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe3_s   <= csa_s;\n        pipe3_c   <= csa_c;\n        pipe3_max <= pipe2_max;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R4: carry-propagate add + absolute value   (pipe3 -> pipe4)\n    // ********************\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] sum_pos;\n    ks_add #(.W(ROW_W)) u_cpa (.a(pipe3_s), .b(pipe3_c), .cin(1'b0), .s(sum_pos));\n    // negated sum: -S = ~s + ~c + 2  (exact in Z/(2^19))\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] cn2 = ~pipe3_c + 19'd2;\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] sum_neg;\n    ks_add #(.W(ROW_W)) u_cnan (.a(~pipe3_s), .b(cn2), .cin(1'b0), .s(sum_neg));\n    wire p4sign = sum_pos[ROW_W-1];\n    wire [ROW_W-1:0] sum_abs = p4sign ? sum_neg : sum_pos;\n\n    logic [SUM_W-1:0] pipe4_sum_c;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe4_max;\n    logic pipe4_sign;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe4_sum_c <= sum_abs;\n        pipe4_max   <= pipe3_max;\n        pipe4_sign  <= p4sign;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R5: normalize + final exponent   (pipe4 -> pipe5)\n    // ********************\n    localparam int unsigned LZC_W = pdpu_pkg::clog2(SUM_W);\n    wire [LZC_W-1:0] lz;\n    wire lz_zero;\n    lzc #(.WIDTH(SUM_W), .MODE(1'b1)) u_lzc (.in_i(pipe4_sum_c), .cnt_o(lz), .empty_o(lz_zero));\n\n    // fre = pipe4_max + (6 - lz); the lzc_zeroes case does not matter (result is 0)\n    wire [LZC_W-1:0] lz6 = ~lz + {{(LZC_W-1){1'b0}}, 2'b10};\n    wire signed [EXP_WIDTH:0] fre;\n    ks_add #(.W(EXP_WIDTH+1)) u_fre (\n        .a(pipe4_max),\n        .b({lz6[LZC_W-1], lz6}),\n        .cin(1'b1),\n        .s(fre)\n    );\n\n    wire [SUM_W-1:0] sum_norm;\n    barrel_shifter #(\n        .WIDTH(SUM_W),\n        .SHIFT_WIDTH(LZC_W),\n        .MODE(1'b0)\n    ) u_norm_sh (\n        .operand_i(pipe4_sum_c),\n        .shift_amount(lz),\n        .result_o(sum_norm)\n    );\n\n    wire sticky = |sum_norm[SUM_W-MANT_WIDTH_O-3-:6];\n    wire [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] fman = {sum_norm[SUM_W-1:SUM_W-MANT_WIDTH_O-2], sticky};\n\n    logic [MANT_WIDTH_O+2:0] pipe5_mant;\n    logic [EXP_WIDTH:0] pipe5_re;\n    logic pipe5_sign;\n    always_ff @(posedge clk) begin\n        pipe5_mant <= fman;\n        pipe5_re   <= fre;\n        pipe5_sign <= pipe4_sign;\n    end\n\n    // ********************\n    // R6: encode   (pipe5 -> out)\n    // ********************\n    wire [n_o-1:0] res;\n    posit_encoder #(\n        .n(n_o),\n        .es(es_o),\n        .EXP_WIDTH(EXP_WIDTH),\n        .MANT_WIDTH(MANT_WIDTH_O+2)\n    ) u_posit_encoder (\n        .sign_i(pipe5_sign),\n        .rg_exp_i(pipe5_re),\n        .mant_norm_i(pipe5_mant),\n        .result_o(res)\n    );\n    assign result_o = res;\n\nendmodule\n", filename='design7.v')

## Summary

**Best result: `design5.v` — 780,015 ADP** (delay 883 ps, area 883), vs. the provided `starting_point.v` baseline of 968,045 (delay 910 ps, area 1063). All designs were verified bit-exact on the 2M-vector testbench.

### 1. Approaches tried
| File | Idea | Result |
|---|---|---|
| `starting_point.v` | upstream 6-register baseline | PASS 968k |
| `design1.v` | algorithmic restructure (see §2) | PASS **824k** |
| `design2.v` | pipeline rebalance, diff in R2 | 1050 ps, worse; rebalanced → PASS **808k** |
| `design4.v` | + Kogge-Stone prefix adders/OR-scan decode | PASS 885k (max+17 subs in one region too slow) |
| `design5.v` | design4 + aggressive stage rebalance, 19-bit rows | PASS **780k** ← best |
| `design6.v` | prefix comparator max tree + shift split across register | PASS 849k (area regression) |
| `design7.v` | + parallel-absolute-value, merged exponent add, restructured encoder | FAIL (encoder) |

### 2. Optimizations with most impact
- **Sign handling reformulation** (design1): replaced 17 per-lane two's-complement ripple adders with bit-wise XOR-folding of each aligned row plus *one* popcount-derived correction row (−k·2¹⁴+k, mod 2¹⁹) folded into the Wallace tree — exact, no adder chain.
- **Booth/CSA multipliers → plain 4×4 multiplies**: the mantissa datapath is exactly an 8-bit unsigned product; removed ~all booth encoder/partial-product/CSA area.
- **Stage rebalancing** (design5): moved the exponent subtract+clamp+align-hi-shift into R3 and the fold+compress+carry-propagate add into R4; shortened rows to 19 bits (result provably fits).
- **Parallel-prefix arithmetic** (`ks_add`, `or_scan`): Kogge-Stone for exponent add, subtract, CPA, final-exponent add, and a merged encoder round/negate add (saves one adder); OR-scan replaces the decoder's negate adder.

### 3. What didn't work / regressions
- Stacking maxtree + 17 subtractors in one region (design4) — 1033 ps critical path.
- `max2p` comparator sign case inverted (fixed: select the *positive* operand), and reading a 17th element from a 16-entry registered vector (out-of-range slice → zeros) — both caught only by simulation.
- design7's encoder restructure (single 31-bit barrel replacing regime adders) is still incorrect — left as the open thread; `design6`'s conservative encoder would be the fallback to graft onto design5's datapath.
- Debugging constraint discovered: Verilator aborts at the first `TB_ERROR`, and only the *head* of stdout is shown on failure — so I embedded a self-test (`test7.v`) printing verdicts at t=0 with a dummy top ($finish/$fatal break Yosys/lint).

### 4. Lessons learned
- Verify every indexing assumption on packed flat vectors (off-by-one slices compile silently and produce all-zero data).
- The eval harness's stdout head (not tail) is the debugging channel; keep prints early and never call `$finish`.
- Next steps if resumed: fix design7's encoder (compare against design6's encoder in isolation), then sweep `target_delay` (400/600 ps) around design5, and try speculative carry-select for the final CPA.
